return ``; } function renderIcons() { document.querySelectorAll('[data-lucide]').forEach(el => { const name = el.getAttribute('data-lucide'); const style = el.getAttribute('style') || ''; const wMatch = style.match(/width:\s*([\d.]+)px/); const h = hMatch ? parseFloat(hMatch[1]) : 20; const c = cMatch ? cMatch[1].trim() : 'currentColor'; const sw = swMatch ? parseFloat(swMatch[1]) : 2; el.outerHTML = svgIcon(name, w, h, c, sw); }); } // Init icons - called after all definitions above are in place renderIcons();
